class
 
SalaryBatchStateMachine
 
{
    
@Autowired
    
private

 salaryMapper
;

 redisTemplate
;
    
public
 
void
 
transit
(

,

 targetStatus
,

 action
)
 
{
        
SalaryBatch
 batch 
=
 salaryMapper
.
selectById
(

);
        
int
 current 
=

.
getStatus
();
        
boolean
 valid 
=
 
false
;
        
switch
 
(
current
)
 
{
            
case
 
0
:
                
if
 
(
targetStatus 
==
 
1
 
&&
 
"CONFIRM"
.
equals
(
action
))
 valid

true
;
                
else

(
targetStatus 
==
 
9

"REJECT"
.
equals
(
action
))
 valid 
=
 
true
;

targetStatus

"FUND_ARRIVED"
.

(

))
 valid

;

(

 valid

;

3
:
                
if
 
(
targetStatus 
==
 
4
)
 valid 
=
 
true
;
                
break
;
            
case
 
4
:
                
if
 
(
targetStatus 
==
 
5

"SUBMIT"
.

(

))
 valid

;

(
targetStatus 
==
 
10

"REJECT"
.
equals
(
action
))
 valid 
=
 
true
;
                
break
;
            
case
 
5
:
                
if
 
(
targetStatus 
==
 
6
 
&&
 
"VERIFY_PASS"
.

(
action
))
 valid 
=
 
true
;
                
else

(

11

"VERIFY_FAIL"
.

(

))
 valid

true

 valid

(

(

.

(
